{"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The process of squirting a small amount of water, in the form of a fine spray, into the air/fuel mixture going into a gasoline piston engine, in order to cool the engine cylinders, and, thereby, reduce detonation or knocking in high performance engines, especially when using a high compression ratio."],"tags":["uncountable"],"topics":["aeronautics","aerospace","automotive","aviation","business","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","transport","vehicles"]},{"glosses":["The process of squirting water into the combustion chamber of a jet engine to allow it to produce greater thrust, partly from the production of high-pressure steam and partly from the cooling effect of the water allowing the engine to burn a greater quantity of fuel without overheating."],"tags":["uncountable"],"topics":["aeronautics","aerospace","aviation","business","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]}],"upstream_line":1146480,"upstream_offset":4086244369,"upstream_record_sha256":"3a18bfadfc846ef3de8ae30222d7091ea4f187f1ac371a2b2ecb77bedae87081","word":"water injection"}
